- **drugdev-pipeline-targets-and-drug-likeness**: Use when orienting on a drug discovery project: the pipeline stage by stage with its attrition and where computation actually helps rather than where it is claimed to, targets and modalities from small molecules to biologics and their different constraints, and what makes a molecule a drug — potency, selectivity, exposure and the property filters worth taking seriously. Includes the router for the whole drug discovery software reference. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/medicine-design-software-development/skills/drugdev-pipeline-targets-and-drug-likeness) · ★2)
- **drugdev-protein-structure-docking-and-molecular-dynamics**: Use for structure-based work: protein structure and what a crystal structure does and does not tell you, structure prediction and how to read a confidence score, docking and the gap between pose prediction and affinity ranking, molecular dynamics with force fields and sampling limits, and free energy methods including FEP and where the accuracy actually is. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/medicine-design-software-development/skills/drugdev-protein-structure-docking-and-molecular-dynamics) · ★2)
- **drugdev-qsar-admet-generative-models-and-validation**: Use for the machine learning layer: QSAR and property prediction, ADMET prediction and its applicability domain, generative models for molecule design and what they actually produce, active learning and the design-make-test-analyse loop, the benchmarks and why leaderboard performance transfers so poorly, and validation that means something — prospective tests, baselines and the checks that catch a leaking model. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/medicine-design-software-development/skills/drugdev-qsar-admet-generative-models-and-validation) · ★2)
- **drugdev-reference**: Use when correcting a drug discovery software misconception, looking up an attrition rate, dataset size, accuracy or timeline figure, finding the tools and resources, or needing a quick-reference picker — plus the current state of AI drug discovery clinical evidence and the FDA and EMA regulatory framework for AI. Companion to the other drug discovery software skills. Use whenever the user asks to check/compare/audit two DSH versions or whether upgrading is safe — e.g. "more changes or reverts in dsh-vX -&gt; dsh-vY", "compare the breaking changes" — even with only two version numbers and no source location. Read-only outside the report directory; npm mode installs in isolation with --ignore-scripts. ([oh-my-dsh/dsh-plugin-upgrade-skill](https://github.com/oh-my-dsh/dsh-plugin-upgrade-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/dsh-upgrade-audit) · ★136)
- **dsh-upstream-first-debug**: 在 DeepSeek Harness（DSH）中，社区开源工具、插件、MCP 或依赖在本地安装、启动、调用、测试中首次报错或出现异常结果时，立即先核对实际运行版本、最新发布版及修复记录，并检索上游 Issues/PR/Discussions；完成核对前不自行修补。适用于第三方开源组件排障，不用于自研业务代码的一般调试。 ([Tzhen-X/dsh-upstream-first-debug](https://github.com/Tzhen-X/dsh-upstream-first-debug/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **dsh-vision-router**: 给纯文本 DeepSeek Harness Agent 装上"眼睛"：粘贴/上传图片即可识别，内置免费视觉链与多模态模型路由，支持看图问答、OCR、元素定位、像素对比、取色、抠图、SVG 矢量化等深度视觉工具。本技能为上游插件的封装。 ([Jaywang1013/Awesome-Himice-SOP-skill](https://github.com/Jaywang1013/Awesome-Himice-SOP-skill/tree/HEAD/integrations/deepseek-harness/dsh-vision-router) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **dsh-weixin-clawbot**: Install, configure, validate, or troubleshoot the community DSH Weixin ClawBot bridge that connects Tencent's official iLink channel to DeepSeek Harness. Use for phone-to-computer DSH setup, pairing, profile installation, session commands, updates, or compatibility checks; not for OpenClaw's own Weixin plugin or desktop-Weixin injection. ([zp-home/dsh-weixin-clawbot](https://github.com/zp-home/dsh-weixin-clawbot/tree/HEAD/skills/dsh-weixin-clawbot) · ★3)
- **dsh-workflow**: Map for using Matt Pocock's engineering skills inside DeepSeek Harness — which of the 36 skills the model may load itself, which are user-only, how the flows connect, and how they hand off to DSH's subagents, plan mode, goals and session commands. Load this before routing work through the mattpocock set, or when a Matt Pocock skill tells you to run something you cannot run. ([NmouZh/dsh-mattpocock-skills](https://github.com/NmouZh/dsh-mattpocock-skills/tree/HEAD/overlay/skills/dsh-workflow/dsh-workflow))
- **dsiprouter**: Call the dSIPRouter REST API using the Postman collection (curl + jq). ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/dsiprouter-skill) · ★15)
- **dskask**: Compatibility shim for the legacy async DeepSeek delegation skill from claude-ccb-skills. Use only when the user explicitly delegates to DeepSeek. ([LeoLin990405/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/LeoLin990405/claude-code-skills/tree/HEAD/ai-collaboration/dskask) · ★1)
- **dsl**: Build and maintain a Domain-Shared Lexicon, a glossary of domain terms that you and the AI agree on. Use when the user says "build a lexicon", "we keep using term X differently", "set up a glossary", "what does Y mean in this project", or whenever you notice the same word being used for two different concepts in conversation. Also activates at session start when a lexicon file is present. ([p-vbordei/dsl](https://github.com/p-vbordei/dsl/tree/HEAD/skills/dsl))
